Use when asked to generate, create, draw, render, illustrate, design, mock up or edit an image, illustration, photo, icon, logo concept, banner, hero image, wallpaper or app mockup with ChatGPT Image, GPT Image, gpt-image, DALL-E or OpenAI, or when a generated raster image is wanted and no other image tool is named. Also for editing existing images: restyle, combine reference images, inpain… ([sdruckerfig/chatgpt-image](https://github.com/sdruckerfig/chatgpt-image/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
